## problem (what fails for the user)
the active-transport snapshot is the gate's transport truthfulness surface; if capture()
infers zeros for unreported AVIO state, transport health receipts become unfalsifiable.

## reproducer
- repo: goreda/gorav @ 91a359f7b38cc0e6c4974661d85f389ef6041b1f (branch main).
- real source: Sources/GorAVFFmpegAdapter/ActiveTransportSnapshot.swift (capture() excerpt below).
- the arch-196s H0594 return validated a MODEL of capture() semantics; its acceptance line
  "replays green against the pinned snapshot producer" stayed OPEN for want of an AVIOContext
  fixture. This refill runs capture() against a real AVIOContext built on avio_alloc_context
  with a scripted read callback and a real av_malloc 64-byte input buffer: bytes fed, drained-to-EOF, and drained-to-error cases.

## expected behaviour
nil rules hold against real AVIO state: a not-reported value stays nil, never inferred;
reported zero is 0, not nil; eofObserved is nil until AVIO observes EOF; a negative AVIO
error surfaces, non-negative stays nil. Needs FFmpeg headers (CFFmpeg): Linux lane with the
full-codec provider runs it; environmental file-only builds cannot.

```text id=rf1
import XCTest
import CFFmpeg
import GorAVNativeProbeKit
@testable import GorAVFFmpegAdapter

// transport-capture-tests (H0594 refill, arch-196y item 4d): run capture()
// against a REAL AVIOContext fixture (avio_alloc_context + scripted read
// callback) instead of a packet-rule model. Asserts the pinned nil rules:
// a not-reported value stays nil, never inferred; negative bytes_read -> nil;
// eof_reached 0 -> nil; error >= 0 -> nil; nil context -> every field nil.

private final class Feed {
    var chunks: [UInt8]
    var pos = 0
    var eofAfter = true          // return AVERROR_EOF when drained
    var errorAfter: Int32? = nil // return this negative code when drained instead
    init(_ bytes: [UInt8]) { chunks = bytes }
}

private func makeCtx(_ feed: Feed) -> UnsafePointer<AVIOContext>? {
    // Real 64-byte input buffer via av_malloc (freed by avio_context_free's
    // av_free pairing): the buffered path is what capture() reports on.
    let opaque = Unmanaged.passRetained(feed).toOpaque()
    let readBlock: @convention(c) (UnsafeMutableRawPointer?, UnsafeMutablePointer<UInt8>?, Int32) -> Int32 = { op, buf, size in
        let f = Unmanaged<Feed>.fromOpaque(op!).takeUnretainedValue()
        let remaining = f.chunks.count - f.pos
        if remaining <= 0 {
            if let e = f.errorAfter { return e }
            return f.eofAfter ? -541478725 /* AVERROR_EOF */ : 0
        }
        let n = min(Int(size), remaining)
        if let buf = buf {
            UnsafeMutableRawBufferPointer(start: buf, count: n).copyBytes(from: f.chunks[f.pos..<(f.pos + n)])
        }
        f.pos += n
        return Int32(n)
    }
    let bufSize: Int32 = 64
    guard let raw = av_malloc(Int(bufSize)) else { return nil }
    let buffer = raw.assumingMemoryBound(to: UInt8.self)
    let ctx = avio_alloc_context(buffer, bufSize, 0, opaque, readBlock, nil, nil)
    return UnsafePointer(ctx)
}

final class TransportCaptureTests: XCTestCase {
    private let src = try! SourceToken(instanceID: 7, openGeneration: 1)
    private let ses = try! SeekSessionID(rawValue: 11)
    private let rev = try! SeekRevision(rawValue: 3)

    func testNilContextYieldsAllNil() {
        let s = ActiveTransportSnapshot.capture(nil, source: src, session: ses, revision: rev)
        XCTAssertNil(s.bytesRead); XCTAssertNil(s.bufferAvailableBytes)
        XCTAssertNil(s.eofObserved); XCTAssertNil(s.errorCode)
        XCTAssertEqual(s.source, src); XCTAssertEqual(s.session, ses); XCTAssertEqual(s.revision, rev)
    }

    func testUnreadContextReportsZeroBytesNotNil() {
        let feed = Feed(Array(repeating: 0xAB, count: 100))
        let ctx = makeCtx(feed)!
        defer {
            Unmanaged<Feed>.fromOpaque(ctx.pointee.opaque!).release()
            var freeing: UnsafeMutablePointer<AVIOContext>? = UnsafeMutablePointer(mutating: ctx)
            avio_context_free(&freeing)
        }
        let s = ActiveTransportSnapshot.capture(ctx, source: src, session: ses, revision: rev)
        XCTAssertEqual(s.bytesRead, 0)          // reported zero is a value, not absence
        XCTAssertNil(s.eofObserved)             // no EOF observed yet
        XCTAssertNil(s.errorCode)
    }

    func testReadAdvancesBytesReadAndBuffer() {
        let feed = Feed(Array(repeating: 0xCD, count: 100))
        let ctx = makeCtx(feed)!
        defer {
            Unmanaged<Feed>.fromOpaque(ctx.pointee.opaque!).release()
            var freeing: UnsafeMutablePointer<AVIOContext>? = UnsafeMutablePointer(mutating: ctx)
            avio_context_free(&freeing)
        }
        var tmp = [UInt8](repeating: 0, count: 40)
        let n = tmp.withUnsafeMutableBytes { avio_read(UnsafeMutablePointer(mutating: ctx), $0.baseAddress!.assumingMemoryBound(to: UInt8.self), 40) }
        XCTAssertEqual(n, 40)
        let s = ActiveTransportSnapshot.capture(ctx, source: src, session: ses, revision: rev)
        // Buffered AVIO truth: the 40-byte read triggered one fill of the
        // 64-byte buffer (feed has 100), so bytes_read = 64 and 24 bytes
        // remain in the user-space buffer.
        XCTAssertEqual(s.bytesRead, 64)
        XCTAssertEqual(s.bufferAvailableBytes, 24)
        XCTAssertNil(s.eofObserved)
    }

    func testEofObservedAfterDraining() {
        let feed = Feed(Array(repeating: 0xEF, count: 10))
        let ctx = makeCtx(feed)!
        defer {
            Unmanaged<Feed>.fromOpaque(ctx.pointee.opaque!).release()
            var freeing: UnsafeMutablePointer<AVIOContext>? = UnsafeMutablePointer(mutating: ctx)
            avio_context_free(&freeing)
        }
        var tmp = [UInt8](repeating: 0, count: 64)
        _ = tmp.withUnsafeMutableBytes { avio_read(UnsafeMutablePointer(mutating: ctx), $0.baseAddress!.assumingMemoryBound(to: UInt8.self), 64) }
        let s = ActiveTransportSnapshot.capture(ctx, source: src, session: ses, revision: rev)
        XCTAssertEqual(s.eofObserved, true)     // observed EOF is reported true, never inferred earlier
    }

    func testTransportErrorSurfaced() {
        let feed = Feed([]); feed.errorAfter = -5 /* EIO-style negative */
        let ctx = makeCtx(feed)!
        defer {
            Unmanaged<Feed>.fromOpaque(ctx.pointee.opaque!).release()
            var freeing: UnsafeMutablePointer<AVIOContext>? = UnsafeMutablePointer(mutating: ctx)
            avio_context_free(&freeing)
        }
        var tmp = [UInt8](repeating: 0, count: 8)
        _ = tmp.withUnsafeMutableBytes { avio_read(UnsafeMutablePointer(mutating: ctx), $0.baseAddress!.assumingMemoryBound(to: UInt8.self), 8) }
        let s = ActiveTransportSnapshot.capture(ctx, source: src, session: ses, revision: rev)
        XCTAssertNotNil(s.errorCode)
        XCTAssertTrue((s.errorCode ?? 0) < 0)
    }
}
```

```swift id=source1
import Foundation
import GorAVNativeProbeKit
import CFFmpeg

/// A queue-time observation of the active AVIOContext, not a cache model.
/// nil means unavailable/not observed, never zero. No paths, URLs or headers.
public struct ActiveTransportSnapshot: Equatable, Sendable {
    public let source: SourceToken
    public let session: SeekSessionID
    public let revision: SeekRevision
    /// FFmpeg AVIO bytes_read counter, cumulative for this open. Includes probe
    /// reads and rereads; not network-only unique bytes or file position.
    public let bytesRead: Int64?
    /// Bytes remaining in AVIO's current user-space input buffer only.
    public let bufferAvailableBytes: Int?
    /// true when AVIO has observed EOF; nil before that or without an AVIOContext.
    /// A seek can clear this observation. No prediction that the remote is finished.
    public let eofObserved: Bool?
    /// Negative FFmpeg AVIO error when present; nil means no error observed,
    /// not an assertion that transport is healthy or unable to stall.
    public let errorCode: Int32?

    // Called only inside the format owner's native queue, never retains pb.
    static func capture(_ pb: UnsafePointer<AVIOContext>?, source: SourceToken,
                        session: SeekSessionID, revision: SeekRevision) -> Self {
        guard let pb else {
            return Self(source: source, session: session, revision: revision,
                        bytesRead: nil, bufferAvailableBytes: nil, eofObserved: nil, errorCode: nil)
        }
        let count = pb.pointee.bytes_read
        let available = gorav_avio_buffer_available(pb)
        return Self(source: source, session: session, revision: revision,
                    bytesRead: count >= 0 ? count : nil,
                    bufferAvailableBytes: available >= 0 ? Int(available) : nil,
                    eofObserved: pb.pointee.eof_reached != 0 ? true : nil,
                    errorCode: pb.pointee.error < 0 ? pb.pointee.error : nil)
    }
}

public enum ActiveTransportRefusal: Error, Equatable, Sendable {
    case notOpen, staleBinding
}

```

```text id=fixtures1
fixture feed scripts (driven by the suite's scripted read callback through avio_alloc_context):
F-zero: 100 bytes staged, no read issued -> bytes_read reported 0 (a value, not nil), eofObserved nil, errorCode nil
F-read: 100 bytes staged, 64-byte AVIO buffer, avio_read of 40 -> one 64-byte fill: bytes_read 64, bufferAvailableBytes 24, eofObserved nil
F-eof:  10 bytes staged, avio_read of 64 drains past end (callback returns AVERROR_EOF) -> eofObserved true
F-err:  0 bytes staged, callback returns -5 -> errorCode < 0 surfaces
F-nil:  nil AVIOContext -> every field nil, source/session/revision echoed

```
